**[00:25:22.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000233)  
mismo ng ating Pangulo

**[00:25:24.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000234)  
nung nakaraang taon.

**[00:25:26.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000235)  
According to the Ombudsman,

**[00:25:29.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000236)  
that's the reason why

**[00:25:30.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000237)  
we would want to cure

**[00:25:32.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000238)  
this conditional veto.

**[00:25:35.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000239)  
That is why we would like to

**[00:25:37.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000240)  
cure it in the 2027

**[00:25:40.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000241)  
GA.

**[00:25:43.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000242)  
It is precisely the problem.

**[00:25:46.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000243)  
You cannot cure

**[00:25:47.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000244)  
through the budget

**[00:25:49.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000245)  
and amend a special law

**[00:25:52.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000246)  
through a general law.

**[00:25:54.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000247)  
This is not on.

**[00:25:55.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000248)  
And that is precisely

**[00:25:56.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000249)  
the ground

**[00:25:57.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000250)  
for the veto message

**[00:25:59.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000251)  
of the President.

**[00:26:00.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000252)  
I hope you consider this

**[00:26:02.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000253)  
because it's clearly a problem.

**[00:26:06.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000254)  
And just repeating it

**[00:26:08.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000255)  
again and again

**[00:26:09.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000256)  
in the budget

**[00:26:10.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000257)  
does not cure

**[00:26:12.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000258)  
the fundamental defect

**[00:26:14.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000259)  
that a budget special provision

**[00:26:17.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000260)  
hidden in the long list

**[00:26:19.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000261)  
of Ombudsman budget

**[00:26:21.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000262)  
can amend a special law.

**[00:26:24.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000263)  
Thank you.

**[00:26:26.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000264)  
Yes, Your Honor, Mr. President.

**[00:26:27.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000265)  
We'd like to

**[00:26:27.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000266)

**[00:46:14.250] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000384)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:46:15.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000384-split-2)  
Mr. President, I move that we consider the budget of the Commission on Elections and recognize the distinguished Senator Panfilo Lacson to sponsor.

**[00:46:27.390] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000384-split-3)  
Hearing no objection,

**[00:46:30.000] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000385)  
Considering the budget of the Commission on Elections, and Senator Pampilo Lacson is recognized.

**[00:46:36.920] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000386)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[00:46:38.820] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000387)  
Mr. President, esteemed colleagues,

**[00:46:41.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000388)  
as Vice Chairman of Subcommittee J of the Committee on Finance,

**[00:46:45.760] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000389)  
it is my privilege to sponsor the proposed budget of the Commission on Elections

**[00:46:49.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000390)  
for fiscal year 2027, amounting to 29,163,661,000 pesos.

**[00:47:00.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000391)  
This represents an increase of 2,000,000 pesos in capital outlay

**[00:47:05.120] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000392)  
over the amount proposed under the National Expenditure Program,

**[00:47:09.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000393)  
which was retained and adopted in the House General Appropriations Bill,

**[00:47:13.660] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000394)  
which stood at 29,161,661,000 pesos.

**[00:47:19.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000395)  
Of the additional allocation, Mr. President,

**[00:47:22.120] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000396)  
1,000,000 is to augment funds for the construction of the Comelec Integrated Field Office buildings,

**[00:47:29.060] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000397)  
while the...

**[00:47:30.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000398)  
either 1,000,000 is allotted or allocated as additional funding

**[00:47:34.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000399)  
for the construction of the new Comelec building.

**[00:47:38.700] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000400)  
The new appropriations will be broken down as follows.

**[00:47:42.200] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000401)  
5,105,751,000 pesos for personal services,

**[00:47:48.920] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000402)  
24,055,910,000 pesos for MOOE,

**[00:47:55.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000403)  
and 2,000,000 pesos for capital outlay.

**[00:48:00.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000404)  
Mr. President, our colleagues may be wondering

**[00:48:01.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000405)  
what 2, 1,000,000 pesos

**[00:48:03.700] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000406)  
for A, construction of the new Comelec building,

**[00:48:09.560] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000407)  
and B, construction of the Comelec Integrated Field Office buildings

**[00:48:13.620] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000408)  
do or contribute to the described line items.

**[00:48:17.320] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000409)  
Let me explain, Mr. President, very briefly.

**[00:48:20.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000410)  
The agency, through the good Comelec chairman,

**[00:48:22.160] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000411)  
is fully mindful of the tight fiscal space

**[00:48:25.400] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000412)  
even going to the following year, 2027,

**[00:48:29.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000413)  
exacerbated by a bad economic outlook.

**[00:48:32.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000414)  
That's why we are putting in nominal amounts of 1,000,000 each.

**[00:48:37.400] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000415)  
So the Comelec, through the chairman,

**[00:48:39.580] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000416)  
being a constitutional body,

**[00:48:41.160] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000417)  
may be authorized to realign, to augment from savings

**[00:48:45.580] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000418)  
or any available appropriations in future budget measures,

**[00:48:50.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000419)  
which we cannot do unless there is a line item.

**[00:48:54.440] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000420)  
In short, Mr. President, and his colleagues,

**[00:48:57.080] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000421)  
In short, Mr. President, colleagues,

**[00:48:59.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000422)  
Gusto lamang po ni Chairman Garcia na magkaroon ng bahay kasi pag wala hong line item, wala siyang paglalagyan. He cannot be authorized to realign or augment. And that is very clear in the Constitution. Having said that, Mr. President, your subcommittee is now ready to accept questions and other points of clarification that our colleagues may wish to ask. Thank you.

**[00:49:22.940] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000422-split-2)  
Any interpellations? Majority Leader.

**[00:49:24.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000422-split-3)  
Mr. President, there are no more reservations

**[00:49:27.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000423)  
to interpolate.

**[00:49:29.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000424)  
The budget of the Commission on Elections.

**[00:49:32.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000425)  
Therefore, I move to close the period of interpolation

**[00:49:34.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000426)  
on the budget of the Commission

**[00:49:36.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000427)  
and that the same be deemed submitted

**[00:49:38.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000428)  
for the body's consideration.

**[00:49:41.340] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000429)  
That is something definite

**[00:49:43.000] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000430)  
because the chairman is not only a friend of mine

**[00:49:46.840] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000431)  
but also a friend of the Depsek of the Senate.

**[00:49:50.380] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000432)  
And then the commissioner is my godson,

**[00:49:55.000] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000433)  
Commissioner Maceda.

**[00:49:55.880] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000434)  
So, definitely,

**[00:49:59.000] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000435)  
no objections.

**[00:49:59.860] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000436)  
The budget of the Commission on Elections

**[00:50:02.240] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000437)  
is hereby dimmed, submitted.

**[00:50:04.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000438)  
Congratulations.

**[00:50:06.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000439)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[00:50:07.000] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000440)  
He just wants to leave early

**[00:50:09.640] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000441)  
instead of being picked up.

**[00:50:24.640] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-000442)  
Session suspended.

**[01:00:30.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-000464)  
President, I move that we consider the budget of the Department of Justice and recognize the distinguished Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan to sponsor and to interpolate the Honorable Senate President Pro Tempore, Senator Tito Soto.

**[01:00:48.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000465)  
The consideration of the budget of the Department of Justice and its attached agencies is now in order for interpolation and to sponsor, the Chair recognizes Senator Francis Pangilinan.

**[01:01:00.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000466)  
Thank you very much, Mr. President.

**[01:01:03.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000467)  
Mr. President, distinguished colleagues, ngayong araw isinusulong natin ang panukalang 2027 budget ng Department of Justice at ang mga attached agencies na ito.

**[01:01:19.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000468)  
Nagkakahalagang 44,283,478,000 pesos.

**[01:01:30.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000469)  
Ang breakdown po nito, as follows.

**[01:01:33.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000470)  
Office of the Secretary, 13.886 billion.

**[01:01:39.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000471)  
Bureau of Corrections, 11.919 billion.

**[01:01:44.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000472)  
Public Attorney's Office, 6.818 billion.

**[01:01:50.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000473)  
National Bureau of Investigation, 3.421 billion.

**[01:01:56.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000474)  
Bureau of Immigration, 2.896 billion.

**[01:02:00.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000475)  
Office of the Solicitor General, 2.006 billion.

**[01:02:06.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000476)  
Land Registration Authority, 1.394 billion.

**[01:02:12.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000477)  
Parole and Probation Administration, 1.186 billion.

**[01:02:18.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000478)  
PCGG, Presidential Commission on Good Government, 326,780.

**[01:02:30.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000479)  
Office of the Government Corporate Council, 296,306,000.

**[01:02:38.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000480)  
Office of the Alternative Dispute Resolution, 134,158,000.

**[01:02:47.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000481)  
Pamumuhunan ang mga pondong ito, investment sa pangako ng saligang batas na paglilingkuran at poprotektahan ang mga karaniwang Pilipinong nagbabayad ng buwis.

**[01:03:00.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000482)  
Malaki ang inaasahan sa DOJ. Mahirap ang mga kasong hinahawakan at kulang sa personel.

**[01:03:10.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000483)  
Dinadagdagan ng inyong subcommittee ng 845 million pesos mula sa HGAB para sa DOJ at attached agency budgets

**[01:03:23.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000484)  
para palakasin ang mga pondo sa hazard pay.

**[01:03:30.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000485)

**[01:35:20.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001086)  
move forward with respect to

**[01:35:23.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001087)  
the concerns raised by the good Senate.

**[01:35:25.460] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001088)  
Don't you think it's also reasonable,

**[01:35:27.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001089)  
to grant the same hazard pay to

**[01:35:29.420] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001090)  
the Bureau of Corrections?

**[01:35:32.560] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001091)  
Yes.

**[01:35:33.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001092)  
That is correct.

**[01:35:35.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001093)  
As of now, the Bureau of Corrections

**[01:35:37.380] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001094)  
personnel do not receive hazard pay.

**[01:35:39.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001095)  
Wala. Yan ang sinasabi natin

**[01:35:41.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001096)  
Mr. President,

**[01:35:42.780] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001097)  
Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:35:48.100] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001098)  
Yes.

**[01:35:49.920] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001099)  
So let's

**[01:35:50.920] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001100)  
discuss this among

**[01:35:53.300] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001101)  
ourselves. And let's find

**[01:35:55.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001102)  
a reasonable,

**[01:35:57.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001103)  
We'll do that, Mr. President, and we understand completely the concern of the good senator from Cavite.

**[01:36:10.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001104)  
It will create that situation wherein others who are likewise deserving of hazard pay may then seek the same set of benefits, Mr. President.

**[01:36:27.480] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001105)  
Thank you. Thank you for being understanding.

**[01:36:30.840] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001106)  
I just hope Director Matibag, our good friend, will not take it against me.

**[01:36:35.500] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001107)  
Thank you. Thank you, Mr. President.

**[01:36:37.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001108)  
Thank you, Senator Lacson.

**[01:36:40.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001109)  
Thank you.

**[01:36:40.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001110)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[01:36:43.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001111)  
Thank you, Senator Lacson, Majority Leader.

**[01:36:45.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001112)  
Next to interpolate, Mr. President.

**[01:36:47.620] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001113)  
Excuse me, Mr. President.

**[01:36:48.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001114)  
Yes, Mr. President.

**[01:36:49.640] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001115)  
We also check with the armed forces.

**[01:36:51.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001116)  
Pareho rin yung standard 540 and 3000.

**[01:36:55.080] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001117)  
So we can also raise their hazard pay for obvious reason, but we should be reasonable with the amount.

**[01:37:02.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001118)  
Thank you.

**[01:37:05.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001120)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[01:37:08.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001121)  
Next to interpolate, Mr. President, will be...

**[01:37:10.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001122)  
Yes, Senator Rafi Tulfo.

**[01:37:13.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001123)  
Yes, Senator Rafi Tulfo is now recognized for his interpellation.

**[01:37:19.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001124)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[01:37:21.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001125)  
Before I start my interpellation, I would like to reiterate my full support of the budget of the DOJ and its attached agencies.

**[01:37:31.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001126)  
Kaya lang meron akong mga katanungan sa tatlong ahensya ng DOJ, attached agency.

**[01:37:37.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001127)  
Number one is OSG, umpisa natin sa OSG.

**[01:37:40.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001128)  
Um, what happened to the Mercedar case?

**[01:37:48.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001129)  
Para dun sa hindi nakakalam ko na itong Mercedar case, ito po yung meron 15 kilometer line na inimposed para sa commercial fishing vessels,

**[01:37:57.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001130)  
big commercial fishing vessels, nang sagay hindi sila makipag-compete sa mga municipal fisher folks.

**[01:38:03.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001131)  
So nag-file ng case sa Supreme Court, itong malilit na fisher folks.

**[01:38:10.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001132)  
And na-dismiss ng Supreme Court dahil na-technical sapagkat hindi nakapag-file on time ng sagot itong OSG.

**[01:38:22.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001133)  
Why?

**[01:38:30.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001134)  
Yes, merong...

**[01:38:33.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001135)  
According to the Solgen, Mr. President, there was an issue on the...

**[01:38:40.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001136)  
prescriptive period that the lower court did not appreciate, and that's why na-dismiss on a technicality.

**[01:38:52.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001137)  
No?

**[01:38:57.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001138)  
And when the Supreme Court ruled on that technicality, the Solgen filed a motion for reconsideration.

**[01:39:06.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001139)  
So, there was a...

**[01:39:09.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001140)  
There was a...

**[01:39:10.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001141)  
controversy, if you can call it that, legal controversy sa lower left, sa RTC.

**[01:39:18.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001142)  
The RTC judge then, who I understand has since retired, did not, at least the contention of the Solgen's office,

**[01:39:28.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001143)  
is that certain rules were disregarded or not followed, and therefore, the lower court said,

**[01:39:40.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001144)  
you filed it out of time.

**[01:39:43.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001145)  
But the Solgen then believed the position of the Solgen was...

**[01:39:48.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001146)  
that was not proper.

**[01:39:51.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001147)  
Therefore, the Supreme Court ruled in favor of the RTC, and then a motion for reconsideration.

**[01:40:01.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001148)  
Correct.

**[01:40:02.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001149)  
Dahil nga na-delay yung pag-file na kaligtaan.

**[01:40:04.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001150)  
So, meron kasi mga nagsabi na...

**[01:40:10.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001151)  
O, taga-OSG, sinadya na i-delay yung pag-file ng sagot sa Supreme Court, kaya na-dismiss.

**[01:40:18.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001152)  
At bakit na sinadya ma-delay dahil nakipag-friendship daw sa mga malalaking commercial fishing vessels?

**[01:40:27.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001153)  
Yes.

**[01:40:30.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001154)  
Yes, this case was in 2023.

**[01:40:35.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001155)  
That is correct.

**[01:40:36.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001156)  
But my own appreciation, based on the...

**[01:40:40.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001157)  
records, is medyo question...

**[01:40:42.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001158)  
This is at least my view as a lawyer.

**[01:40:45.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001159)  
Medyo questionable yung naging ruling ng RTC, actually, in terms of the period within which they could file an appeal.

**[01:40:56.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001160)  
15 days, I think, I believe.

**[01:40:57.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001161)  
Yes.

**[01:40:58.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001162)  
They have...

**[01:40:58.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001163)  
The reckoning, I think, there was a...

**[01:41:00.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001164)  
Based on the records, the reckoning period was so.

**[01:41:06.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001165)  
Pero parang hindi ito...

**[01:41:10.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001166)  
Nirecognize nung RTC, based on the records, kaya na-dismiss yung kaso.

**[01:41:15.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001167)  
And then, umabot sa Supreme Court.

**[01:41:19.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001168)  
But right now nga, meron ng pending motion for reconsideration.

**[01:41:22.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001169)  
Pero hindi man lang pinarusahan ang OSG, yung kanilang personel, na naging dahilan para ma-delay yung pag-file ng motion?

**[01:41:32.440] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001170)  
Ng answer?

**[01:41:34.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001171)  
Kasi, para naman yung mga small fisher folks natin, mapanatang kalooban dahil sabi nila,

**[01:41:39.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001172)  
palibasa malilit kami, wala kaming panlagay, wala kaming pang-PR.

**[01:41:43.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001173)  
Itong mga malalaking commercial fishing vessels may pang-PR, nanuhul.

**[01:41:48.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001174)  
I mean, pasintabi.

**[01:41:50.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001175)  
Kaya nga, sinadyak ngayon ng OSG na i-delay yung pag-file ng sagot.

**[01:41:56.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001176)  
So yun na siguro, para naman ano, kailangan siguro maparusahan o maano man lang,

**[01:42:01.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001177)  
mabigyan ng memo kung sino man yung sa OSG na naging reason para naging delay yung pag-sagot.

**[01:42:07.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001178)  
Yes, I understand the concern of the good senator.

**[01:42:09.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001179)  
In fact, we are hearing this, we are still hearing this in the Committee on Agriculture.

**[01:42:14.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001180)  
Okay.

**[01:42:15.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001181)  
And the good senator attended, as well as Senator Erwin.

**[01:42:20.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001182)  
But my appreciation of the documents when it was presented to me,

**[01:42:24.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001183)  
was that kung merong, of course there is no evidence, we don't have the evidence,

**[01:42:30.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001184)  
but it's speculative, but having said that, just to qualify,

**[01:42:36.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001185)  
parang kung merong nasuhulan, hindi yung soldier,

**[01:42:39.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001186)  
kung hindi yung RTC.

**[01:42:43.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001187)  
Dahil binasa ko yung records, iba yung interpretation ng RTC judge,

**[01:42:52.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001188)  
dun sa interpretation ng soldier, only precisely the technicality of the time within which they could file a motion for...

**[01:43:01.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001189)  
Reconservation.

**[01:43:03.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001190)  
Yes, yes. That is my understanding.

**[01:43:05.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001191)  
Dami nang binasa ko.

**[01:43:08.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001192)  
Mga binabasa kong dokumento.

**[01:43:09.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001193)  
Hindi ko na maalala masyado.

**[01:43:11.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001194)  
But yun ang immediate reaction ko, bakit ginawa ito ng RTC?

**[01:43:16.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001195)  
But having said that, the soldier is of the view that precisely they did not investigate

**[01:43:26.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001196)  
because the issue for them was a conflict in the interpretation of the law,

**[01:43:35.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001197)  
rather than culpability.

**[01:43:39.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001198)  
A probable violation of any individual.

**[01:43:43.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001199)  
Okay, good.

**[01:43:45.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001200)  
Correct me if I'm wrong, sulod-sulod ang talo ng kaso ng BIFAR sa Supreme Court.

**[01:43:51.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001201)  
And anong ginagawa ng soldier?

**[01:43:54.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001202)  
Bakit palaging talunan ang ating mga taga-BIFAR sa mga kaso?

**[01:43:59.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001203)  
Pero before going to that, last na naman pala dun sa Mercedar case, anong pinaka-status?

**[01:44:05.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001204)  
It's still with the Supreme Court.

**[01:44:07.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001205)  
It's pending ang motion for reconsideration.

**[01:44:09.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001206)  
So it's really, right now, the ball is in the court of the Supreme.

**[01:44:14.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001207)  
The ball is in the Supreme Court's court.

**[01:44:17.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001208)  
Okay.

**[01:44:19.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001209)  
Now, let's go to BIFAR.

**[01:44:23.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001210)  
Sulod-sulod natin ang pagkatalo ng mga kaso ng BIFAR sa pagdating Supreme Court,

**[01:44:32.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001211)  
particularly dun sa paggamit ng vessel monitoring system.

**[01:44:36.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001212)  
Bakit palaging natatalo yung BIFAR?

**[01:44:38.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001213)  
Mahina ba yung...

**[01:44:39.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001214)  
Yes, the Solicitor General's office is likewise unhappy with the ruling of the Supreme Court.

**[01:44:57.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001215)  
This representation as well.

**[01:45:00.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001216)  
and therefore, they did file a motion for reconsideration.

**[01:45:05.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001217)  
So, last na lamang, anong reason bakit natalo yung before doon sa vessel monitoring system?

**[01:45:12.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001218)  
So, technical ulit dahil hindi nakapag-motion for reconsideration on time?

**[01:45:18.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001219)  
No, this is a ruling on the merits.

**[01:45:21.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001220)  
On the merits.

**[01:45:21.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001221)  
So, it's not a technicality.

**[01:45:23.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001222)  
Okay.

**[01:45:23.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001223)  
If I recall correctly, very vague, I can recall quite, not exactly the wording,

**[01:45:34.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001224)  
but they were saying that it was a violation of the trade secrets of these commercial fishing vessels.

**[01:45:49.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001225)  
And that meron din privacy.

**[01:45:52.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001226)  
And then it wasn't.

**[01:45:53.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001227)  
Okay.

**[01:45:58.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001228)  
Yes.

**[01:45:58.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001229)  
So, they did favor the commercial fishing vessels petition.

**[01:46:05.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001230)  
And that's why there is now a motion for reconsideration.

**[01:46:09.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001231)  
Okay.

**[01:46:09.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001232)  
So, yan pinaka-status ngayon.

**[01:46:11.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001233)  
Meron motion for reconsideration pending ngayon sa Supreme Court.

**[01:46:14.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001234)  
And we are in the Senate.

**[01:46:16.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001235)  
At least your Committee on Agriculture, sir, is monitoring this

**[01:46:19.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001236)  
because we are likewise interested in the final outcome

**[01:46:23.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001237)  
because we feel that this is a very important issue.

**[01:46:23.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001238)  
And at the moment, Mr. President, if I may add,

**[01:46:28.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001239)  
your Committee on Agriculture likewise would like to inform you

**[01:46:33.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001240)  
that we are revisiting the Fisheries Code

**[01:46:39.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001241)  
and introducing proposed amendments.

**[01:46:42.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001242)  
The Committee report will be out perhaps after the budget debates

**[01:46:48.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001243)  
so that we can strengthen how we can protect our marginal resources.

**[01:46:53.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001244)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[01:46:58.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001245)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[01:46:59.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001246)  
Now, let's move on sa Bucor.

**[01:47:02.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001247)  
Okay.

**[01:47:03.400] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001248)  
Recently, in the past few months,

**[01:47:06.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001249)  
sunod-sunod yung pagpupunta sa akin ng mga PDL na kalalaya lang

**[01:47:10.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001250)  
after 20 years or 30 years nag-serve sa penitentiary.

**[01:47:16.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001251)  
And wala man lang binigay sa kanilang pampamasahe.

**[01:47:20.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001252)  
Walang-wala.

**[01:47:21.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001253)  
Zero yung sila.

**[01:47:22.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001254)  
They don't know what to do.

**[01:47:23.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001255)  
Kasi kung ganun ang treatment natin sa mga napalaya natin ng mga PDL

**[01:47:28.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001256)  
after so many years,

**[01:47:30.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001257)  
eh baka bumalik sila ulit sa masamang gawain

**[01:47:32.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001258)  
kasi walang-wala.

**[01:47:33.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001259)  
Wala ba tayong mga halfway houses tulad sa ibang bansa

**[01:47:36.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001260)  
para tinutulungan sila?

**[01:47:41.860] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001261)  
And then, correct me if I'm wrong,

**[01:47:44.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001262)  
dati ata meron tayong tinatawag or sa ibang bansa kuwata na mga ito.

**[01:47:48.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001263)  
Yung bawat PDL, habang nasa loob sila,

**[01:47:51.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001264)  
meron silang sweldo.

**[01:47:52.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001265)  
Doon sa pagtatrabaho nila sa loob,

**[01:47:54.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001266)  
regardless kung anong klaseng trabaho binibigay sa kanila,

**[01:47:56.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001267)  
like one peso or one dollar per day.

**[01:47:59.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001268)  
So if you compute that for 20 years na kapag silbi sila,

**[01:48:02.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001269)  
paglabas sila, meron silang 7,000 US dollars or something pesos.

**[01:48:05.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001270)  
Meron silang 7,000 something pesos.

**[01:48:07.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001271)

**[01:49:29.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001291)

Sorry.

**[01:55:34.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001424)

**[01:55:39.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001425)

**[01:56:13.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001428)

**[01:56:18.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001429)  
Okay.

**[01:56:20.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001430)

**[01:56:30.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001432)

**[01:57:57.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001455)

**[02:29:24.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002094)  
On their mandate in the Department of Agrarian Reform, one of our hard-working departments,

**[02:29:31.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002095)  
and continuously, continuously performing their duties in land transfers to the people in need.

**[02:29:41.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002096)  
So, Mr. President, I move that we take up their budget.

**[02:29:45.300] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-002097)  
Any objection?

**[02:29:48.260] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-002098)  
Hearing none, the budget of the Department of Agrarian Reform is hereby considered.

**[02:29:52.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002099)  
May we recognize that...

**[02:29:54.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002100)  
...equally hard-working chairperson of the subcommittee.

**[02:30:00.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002101)  
No other than Senator Kiko Pangilinan, our resident agriculturist, Mr. President.

**[02:30:05.020] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-002102)  
My favorite nephew-in-law is recognized, Senator Pangilinan.

**[02:30:08.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002103)  
Thank you, Mr. President. Thank you, Majority Leader.

**[02:30:13.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002104)  
Ginoong Pangulo, sinusuportahan po natin ang panukalang budget ng Department of Agrarian Reform para sa 2027 na nagkakahalagang P16,121,735,000.

**[02:30:27.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002105)  
Ang kabuhang pondo ng DAR, Puhunan sa Siguridad sa Lupa, Kabuhayan at Katatagan ng ating mga magsasaka.

**[02:30:37.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002106)  
Dinagdagan natin ng P1,700,000,000 sa bersyong ipinasa ng Kamara.

**[02:30:44.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002107)  
Kabilang dito ang P600,000,000 para sa Value Chain Information for Sustainable Transformation in Agrarian Reform Communities o VISTA.

**[02:30:54.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002108)  
Kabilang din ang P500,000,000.

**[02:30:57.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002109)  
P500,000,000 bilang compensation sa mga may-ari ng lupa na saklaw ng reformang agraryo.

**[02:31:06.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002110)  
VISTA is a six-year initiative from 2025 to 2030 designed to improve the livelihoods of agrarian reform beneficiaries, ARBs,

**[02:31:16.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002111)  
through sustainable agriculture, enterprise development, and rural infrastructure.

**[02:31:21.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002112)  
May pagkahawig sa ating sagipsaka pero nakatutok sa 70,000 small...

**[02:31:27.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002113)  
...holder households sa mahigit 80 agrarian reform communities.

**[02:31:31.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002114)  
Sa harap ng banta ng matinding El Niño, kasama rin sa VISTA ang paghahanda sa ating mga komunidad bago matuyo ang lupa.

**[02:31:40.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002115)  
Palalakasin nito ang supply ng tubig, rainwater collection, agroforestry, at iba pang pamamaraan ng climate adaptation.

**[02:31:50.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002116)  
Kasabay ng mga ito, papabilisin din ng pamamahagi ng lupa at palalakasin ang serbisyong legal...

**[02:31:56.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002117)  
... para makapag-aaral sa lupa.

**[02:31:57.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002118)  
Ang mga makinabang ang dapat makinabang, hindi ang mga makapangyarihang interes, kundi ang ating mga magsasaka.

**[02:32:04.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002119)  
Mr. President, it is also my honor to defend the agrarian reform department budget,

**[02:32:13.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002120)  
considering that the Secretary is my schoolmate from the legendary LaSalle Green Hills.

**[02:32:22.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002121)  
Animal LaSalle.

**[02:32:24.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002122)  
At handa na po tayong...

**[02:32:27.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002123)  
... tumugon sa mga tanong ng ating butihing mga senador. Maraming salamat po.

**[02:32:35.560] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-002124)  
Majority Leader, Sen. Amy Marcos is recognized.

**[02:32:39.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002125)  
Yes, thank you very much, Mr. President, Majority Leader, our Chairman.

**[02:32:44.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002126)  
I simply want an update. As a long-standing agrarian reform advocate, I simply would like an update on the progress.

**[02:32:53.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002127)  
We have reported time and again that the number of beneficiaries...

**[02:32:57.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002128)  
... from the condonation effort in 2023 was at 620,255 ARBs.

**[02:33:05.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002129)  
Ilan na ba ngayon at ilan pa ba ang pending?

**[02:33:08.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002130)  
Kasi ang alam ko, may balance pa rin tayo dun sa 57.76 billion na target natin.

**[02:33:16.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002131)  
Parang kulang-kulang pa ng 3 billion worth. Tama ba yun?

**[02:33:20.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002132)  
At ano ang plano natin para matapos na yan?

**[02:33:27.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002133)  
According to the Secretary, ang nakitira na namang ay ang nasa BARM.

**[02:33:35.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002134)  
Dahil hinihintay pa nila.

**[02:33:39.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002135)  
Ahinintay lang nila yung eleksyon. At since tapos na...

**[02:33:42.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002136)  
Tapos na.

**[02:33:45.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002137)  
Yung buong 3 billion, lahat BARM yun. Malaki-laki to ha. 283,387 hectares. Lahat yan BARM?

**[02:33:53.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002138)  
That is correct, according to the Secretary.

**[02:33:55.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002139)  
That entire balance.

**[02:33:57.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002140)  
So we are awaiting BARM. And we look forward to the completion of this program.

**[02:34:05.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002141)  
So yun yung update, yung isa pang update sana, yung tungkol sa VISTA project.

**[02:34:11.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002142)  
Yung VISTA project, yung Senate, through the Chair, restored the 600 million reduction of the House.

**[02:34:18.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002143)  
Ayan. Eto na yung mga foreign assistant projects na binabas bawatan.

**[02:34:23.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002144)  
Para lang maintindihan natin at bakit rin restore ng House.

**[02:34:27.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002145)  
What is the purpose of the project? It's a distinction from other existing livelihood and support projects.

**[02:34:34.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002146)  
Ano yung kanyang pakay?

**[02:34:41.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002147)  
Actually, the VISTA, Value Chain Innovation for Sustainable Transformation in Agrarian Reform, precisely addresses the challenges of the supply chain.

**[02:34:52.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002148)  
Oo nga.

**[02:34:53.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002149)  
Dahil pag buhag-hag yung supply chain,

**[02:34:57.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002150)  
hindi maibibenta ng tama, hindi maibibenta sa tamang presyo,

**[02:35:01.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002151)  
o hindi maibibenta, titatapo na lamang ang ating mga produkto.

**[02:35:06.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002152)  
So, they have target beneficiaries of 80,000 smallholder households,

**[02:35:12.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002153)  
and 400,000 individual beneficiaries.

**[02:35:16.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002154)  
50% of their beneficiaries are women, 30% are IMPs, and 20% kabataan.

**[02:35:22.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002155)  
To reduce rural poverty and increase food security,

**[02:35:26.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002156)  
while protecting and enhancing the natural ecosystems.

**[02:35:30.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002157)  
And of course, the clear objective is to increase income and employment,

**[02:35:35.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002158)  
and climate resilience of target groups in fragile upland areas,

**[02:35:41.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002159)  
including women, youth, and IPs,

**[02:35:43.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002160)  
through the strengthening of inclusive value chains,

**[02:35:47.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002161)  
with conservation and sustainable use of natural resources.

**[02:35:50.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002162)  
Sorry, it sounds very much like very similar livelihood and support services.

**[02:35:56.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002163)  
So, is it different?

**[02:35:57.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002164)

**[02:35:59.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002165)  
It is concentrated on two regions, namely CAR and Region 12,

**[02:36:04.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002166)

**[02:36:11.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002167)  
Yes, it is focused on two regions.

**[02:36:15.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002168)  
This would be CAR and Region 12.

**[02:36:18.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002169)

**[02:36:19.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002170)  
Sorry.

**[02:36:20.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002171)  
Region CAR.

**[02:36:22.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002172)  
CAR, okay. Cordillera and Region 12.

**[02:36:25.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002173)  
And Region 12.

**[02:36:26.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002174)  
That sucks our genes.

**[02:36:27.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002175)  
Yes.

**[02:36:27.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002176)  
And focused likewise on two principal crops,

**[02:36:33.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002177)

**[02:36:36.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002178)  
So, this is the focus, apart from the other programs.

**[02:36:42.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002179)

July was released this year.

**[02:37:49.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002199)

**[02:37:54.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002200)  
Okay.

**[02:37:55.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002201)

**[02:37:56.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002202)

**[02:37:58.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002203)  
92%.

**[02:37:59.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002204)  
Yes.

**[02:38:00.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002205)  
Okay.

**[02:38:01.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002206)  
So, I think the Senate was right in restoring the 600 million.

**[02:38:06.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002207)

And because of...

**[02:38:48.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002219)  
So, the reason for the delay primarily was because of COVID, the pandemic.

**[02:38:54.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002220)  
The COVID.

**[02:38:55.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002221)  
Yes.

**[02:38:55.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002222)  
Yes.

**[02:38:56.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002223)  
I also understand that quite a few documents had to be redone simply because of the debt

**[02:39:04.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002225)

**[02:39:17.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002231)

And they have been submitting...

**[02:42:17.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002289)  
Kaya nakikibalita lang ako nangyari na ron.

**[02:42:20.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002290)  
Yes.

**[02:42:21.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002291)  
The secretary has assured us that they will look into...

**[02:42:25.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002292)  
Do it and act on it as soon as possible.

**[02:42:27.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002293)  
More importantly, could they report back?

**[02:42:29.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002294)  
Because there have been complaints that nothing was done.

**[02:42:36.520] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002295)  
If you could file, if you could also provide us a copy of whatever investigation or results

**[02:42:42.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002296)  
or any charges that have been filed, administrative, civil, or criminal,

**[02:42:46.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002297)  
para at least masagot naman namin na may ginawa naman yung DAR.

**[02:42:49.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002298)  
Dahil ang sinasabi, wala raw ginawa.

**[02:42:52.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002299)  
The secretary issued a special order.

**[02:42:55.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002300)  
Order number 67, directing the regional director to look into it.

**[02:43:02.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002301)  
Pero that was February pa. Anong kinalabsan?

**[02:43:06.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002302)  
Yes.

**[02:43:07.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002303)  
Yun ang gusto natin maalaman, no?

**[02:43:09.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002304)  
Dahil, on top of that,

**[02:43:12.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002305)  
oo, kung pwedeng bigyan na lang ng report dahil maraming nagsusumbong.

**[02:43:16.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002306)  
Ang problema pa kasi niyan,

**[02:43:19.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002307)  
nasa kapas-tarlak na naman itong Paxilica.

**[02:43:22.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002308)  
At saka, may reklamo na naman.

**[02:43:24.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002309)  
It's intended to cover 4,000 acres originally, tapos binawasan.

**[02:43:31.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002310)  
And then, DAR made a statement that, in fact,

**[02:43:35.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002311)  
it has not received a single application for conversion of agricultural land

**[02:43:40.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002312)  
into commercial or industrial for the proposed project area.

**[02:43:45.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002313)  
So, the question arises, we have this kapas issue of land grabbing.

**[02:43:50.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002314)  
Ngayon naman, ang tinatanong, has there an inventory already?

**[02:43:54.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002315)  
been made confirming whether any portion of the 1,619-hectare site

**[02:44:01.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002316)  
covered by certain ARBs,

**[02:44:05.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002317)  
awarded to ARBs?

**[02:44:07.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002318)  
Meron na ba tayong listahan niyan?

**[02:44:09.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002319)  
Or what's happening there?

**[02:44:13.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002320)  
Because there are allegations already of notices to evict.

**[02:44:17.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002321)  
There are allegations of requests to sign quit claims and waivers including IPs.

**[02:44:24.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002322)  
So, when do you plan to do that?

**[02:44:33.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002323)  
Yes.

**[02:44:34.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002324)  
The good Secretary is assuring us that before the bicam convenes, we will be updated

**[02:44:40.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002325)  
regarding this particular.

**[02:44:41.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002326)  
Or earlier. No, dapat siguro earlier.

**[02:44:45.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002327)  
Yes. But in any case,

**[02:44:48.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002328)  
have safeguards already been in place?

**[02:44:52.630] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002329)  
Or can we get the assurance, um, um, can we get the assurance of our Secretary

**[02:45:00.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002337)  
and the entire department, that the farmers who have been awarded this land

**[02:45:06.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002338)  
will not be displaced, that the land that is agricultural will not be converted

**[02:45:13.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002339)  
from agricultural to whatever it is without proper consultation, restitution,

**[02:45:21.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002340)  
compensation, and proof that sustainable non-agricultural livelihoods will be provided.

**[02:45:26.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002341)  
Yes, the Secretary is expressing his commitment to address these concerns raised by the good Senator.

**[02:45:38.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002342)  
And likewise, as Chairman of the Committee on Agriculture, we assure the good Senator

**[02:45:43.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002343)  
that our committee will likewise look into this and look into the safeguards

**[02:45:48.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002344)  
that the good Senator has suggested so that we can address their concerns.

**[02:45:53.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002345)  
Yes, thank you. And finally,

**[02:45:56.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002346)  
I'm a bit puzzled by this announcement.

**[02:46:02.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002347)  
Why is DAR implementing the P28.243 billion PBBM bridges project?

**[02:46:08.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002348)  
What is DAR's specific mandate and expertise in bridge construction?

**[02:46:21.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002349)  
According to the Secretary, they are not implementing.

**[02:46:26.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002350)  
They are implementing the program, the P28.243 billion bridge program, but...

**[02:46:30.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002351)  
Why is it under them?

**[02:46:32.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002352)  
They have identified the agrarian reform communities.

**[02:46:41.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002353)  
All the ARCDPs are your listings?

**[02:46:44.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002354)  
Yes.

**[02:46:44.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002355)  
So how many bridges are targeted for 2027 and where will they be?

**[02:46:50.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002356)  
Siguro, bigyan mo na lang kami ng listahan.

**[02:46:53.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002358)  
Yes, we will.

**[02:46:55.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002358-split-2)  
So, the draft loan...

**[02:46:56.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002359)  
The draft loan agreement already specifies and identifies all these ARCDPs?

**[02:47:02.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002360)  
Yes.

**[02:47:03.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002361)  
Yes, the...

**[02:47:04.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002362)  
Nandun sa draft loan agreement, yung...

**[02:47:07.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002363)  
Dun sa annexes, yung mga agrarian reform communities that will benefit from the bridge program?

**[02:47:14.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002364)  
What was the criteria of...

**[02:47:15.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002365)  
The answer is yes.

**[02:47:17.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002366)  
Yes.

**[02:47:17.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002367)  
What was the criteria for choosing the bridges?

**[02:47:26.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002400)  
So, they have submitted 780 locations as instructed by the...

**[02:47:32.520] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002401)  
For 2027, lahat yan?

**[02:47:34.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002402)  
No, this is for the five-year program.

**[02:47:38.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002403)  
So, how did you choose?

**[02:47:40.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002404)  
How did you select these ARCDPs?

**[02:47:43.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002405)  
You tell me.

**[02:47:43.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002406)  
What's the prioritization?

**[02:47:45.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002407)  
You tell me and I will tell you.

**[02:47:47.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002408)  
Yes, you tell me.

**[02:47:48.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-002409)  
The IRR, the Economic Internal Rate of Return, has been the basis of NEDA and DEPDEV to identify

**[02:47:54.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-002410)  
and approve.

**[02:47:55.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-002411)  
The set locations that we have submitted.

**[02:47:57.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002412)  
So, it is a DEPDEV that identified the criteria, and based on that criteria, the DAR submitted

**[02:48:10.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002413)  
the proposed number of 700?

**[02:48:16.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002415)  
Yeah, 700.

**[02:48:21.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002417)  
And of the 700 plus that was recommended...

**[02:48:25.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002418)  
300.

**[02:48:25.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002419)  
The NEDA approved 300...

**[02:48:27.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002420)  
DEPDEV approved 350 total.

**[02:48:30.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002421)  
Yes.

**[02:48:30.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002422)  
Okay.

**[02:48:31.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002423)  
Siguro, wala kasi kaming alam tungkol dito at nakakapagtaka pag sinabing DAR, biglang may

**[02:48:38.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002424)  
Bridges Program.

**[02:48:39.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002425)  
Bakit hindi DPWH o di kaya yung PRDP man lang ng DA or something, ano?

**[02:48:45.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002426)  
Kaya nagulat ng lahat.

**[02:48:46.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002427)  
Kaya siguro, ang maaaring yung gawin, ibigay na yung listahan, yung terms of reference,

**[02:48:52.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002428)  
kung ano yun.

**[02:48:53.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002429)  
At...

**[02:48:54.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002430)  
Nandito kasi sa 2027 capital outlay, yung sa budget ninyo, 93.463 million for land outlay.

**[02:49:05.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002431)  
Is this designated for right-of-way and bridge approaches?

**[02:49:10.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002432)  
Ganun ba yun?

**[02:49:11.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002433)  
Yes, that is correct.

**[02:49:12.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002434)  
If we may also inform the good senator that of the 28.24 billion total project cost, because

**[02:49:22.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002435)  
this is a official development aid.

**[02:49:24.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002436)  
Right.

**[02:49:25.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002437)  
The government counterpart for 2027 is 1.4 billion.

**[02:49:33.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002438)  
For that entity?

**[02:49:34.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002439)  
Yes.

**[02:49:34.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002440)  
So, and that's why it is the DAR that is the institution that is now working in partnership

**[02:49:46.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002441)  
with the host LGUs and the DPWH.

**[02:49:51.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002442)  
Oh, I see.

**[02:49:52.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002443)  
It is the DAR because...

**[02:49:54.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002444)  
Sorry.

**[02:50:03.120] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002445)  
Yeah.

**[02:50:03.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002446)  
Mr. President, if I may, to assist our sponsor, because we have that program in Bukidnon.

**[02:50:11.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002447)  
Many of our grand reform communities are actually funded their roads.

**[02:50:16.800] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002448)  
And mind you, they're very good roads and bridges going to the ARCs, the grand reform

**[02:50:21.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002449)  
communities, that were funded by loan programs from DAR.

**[02:50:24.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002450)  
Apparently, DAR has a...

**[02:50:27.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002451)  
There are governments and the financial institutions that look towards these programs for grand

**[02:50:34.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002452)  
reform.

**[02:50:34.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002453)  
So, they top that and they do a better job actually identifying the areas.

**[02:50:41.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002454)  
Hindi siya ghost.

**[02:50:42.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002455)  
Grand reform communities talaga siya.

**[02:50:44.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002456)  
Because, ang nag-recommend is ARC, the communities itself.

**[02:50:48.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002457)  
Pero sabi kasi ni Chairman kanina, ang Dep-Dev ang gumawa ng listahan.

**[02:50:53.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002458)  
Ah, yung criteria yata.

**[02:50:54.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002459)  
Ah, yung criteria.

**[02:50:55.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002460)  
In terms of economic...

**[02:50:57.100] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002461)  
Yeah, only the economic viability of the area.

**[02:51:00.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002462)  
Pero, I just want to put on record, doon sa amin sa Bukidnon, happy-happy sila sa mga developmental

**[02:51:06.280] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002463)  
programs sa DAAR.

**[02:51:06.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002464)  
Maraming nga humihingi, kaya tinatanong ko eh.

**[02:51:10.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002465)  
Yung isa pa kasi dyan, yung unprogrammed appropriations, nakalista na naman siya eh, nasa

**[02:51:17.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002466)  
10.877 billion.

**[02:51:19.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002467)  
What conditions must ensue before the funds can actually be released?

**[02:51:24.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002468)  
Kasi, pag nakakakita tayo ng unprogrammed eh, kinikilabutan na kami dito eh.

**[02:51:39.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002469)  
Is it the standard just to help you along as Senator Subiri did?

**[02:51:43.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002470)  
Kapag dumating yung loan proceeds, saka lang ito maa-activate.

**[02:51:48.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002471)  
Tama?

**[02:51:49.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002472)  
Ganyan usually yan eh.

**[02:51:50.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002473)  
Kaya napupunta sa unprogrammed dahil hindi pa natin natatanggap yung loan proceeds.

**[02:51:54.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002474)  
Yes, well, because it's official development aid, it's placed in the unprogrammed item

**[02:52:04.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002475)  
in our budget.

**[02:52:05.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002476)  
I think that's not the reason.

**[02:52:07.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002477)  
The reason really is, hindi pa dumarating, diba?

**[02:52:11.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002478)  
Saka lang, malalaman kung babayaran.

**[02:52:14.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002479)  
Yes, these are loan proceeds.

**[02:52:16.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002480)  
Oo, loan proceeds need to arrive muna.

**[02:52:18.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002481)  
Yes.

**[02:52:18.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002482)  
And then, it will trigger the release of the unprogrammed.

**[02:52:23.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002483)  
Yes.

**[02:52:24.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002484)  
Hindi pa natatanggap.

**[02:52:24.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002485)  
Hindi pa natatanggap.

**[02:52:24.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002486)  
Hindi pa natatanggap.

**[02:52:24.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002487)  
Ito yung labo-labo na biglang may unprogrammed na nare-release na una pa sa NEP.

**[02:52:29.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002488)  
It is expected.

**[02:52:31.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002489)  
Pero, hindi pa nangyayari.

**[02:52:34.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002490)  
Hindi pa.

**[02:52:34.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002491)  
Ayon.

**[02:52:35.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002492)  
So, that's what I was saying earlier.

**[02:52:36.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002493)  
So, I expected that to be the same.

**[02:52:39.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002494)  
And if any questions arise, dahil nga allergic kami sa unprogrammed, at least that's on record.

**[02:52:45.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002495)  
Maraming salamat.

**[02:52:46.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002496)  
Yun lang naman.

**[02:52:47.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002497)  
Nakikibalita lang naman ako sa DAR.

**[02:52:49.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002498)  
Maraming salamat.

**[02:52:51.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002499)  
Agyaman na ka po, Jossie Agnina, to our good Senator.

**[02:52:54.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002500)  
Senator Aimee.

**[02:52:57.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002501)  
Thank you.

**[02:52:57.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002502)  
Champion of agriculture.

**[02:52:59.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002503)  
Senator, Mr. President, Senator Bongo would like to also have his.

**[02:53:04.340] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-002504)  
Senator Bongo, recognized.

**[02:53:13.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002505)  
Mr. President, good afternoon, distinguished colleagues, and to the Department of Agrarian Reform,

**[02:53:19.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002506)  
led by Secretary Conrado Estrella.

**[02:53:22.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002507)  
Sir, congratulations.

**[02:53:24.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002508)  
Sa inyong magandang ginagawa sa Departamento.

**[02:53:29.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002509)  
Just a few, ano lang po itong manifestation and updates lang po, hinihingi ko lang po, no?

**[02:53:36.240] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002510)  
Mr. President, Republic Act No. 9700 strengthened the Comprehensive Agrarian Reform Program, or CARP.

**[02:53:45.340] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002511)  
This law extended the period for the acquisition and distribution of agricultural lands covered by the program,

**[02:53:54.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002512)  
and instituted necessary reforms to promote social justice, improve agricultural productivity,

**[02:54:00.720] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002513)  
and uplift the lives of our farmers and farm workers.

**[02:54:04.760] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002514)  
I would like to commend the Department of Agrarian Reform, under the leadership of Secretary Conrado Estrella III,

**[02:54:12.840] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002515)  
for distributing more than 460,000 land titles, covering approximately 648,000 hectares of agricultural land,

**[02:54:24.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002516)  
and benefiting more than 432,000 agrarian reform beneficiaries from July 2022 to October 2026.

**[02:54:35.960] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002517)  
Marami po sa ating mga magsasaka ang ilang dekada ng nagsasaka.

**[02:54:41.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002518)  
Ngunit ngayon lamang nabigyan ng pagkakataon magkaroon ng sariling lupa.

**[02:54:46.940] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002519)

**[04:06:44.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-003393)

**[04:06:55.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-003394)

**[04:07:01.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-003395)

Eh, hindi yung ginagawing trabaho nyo na dapat ginagampanan yan dahil kawawa naman yung mga farmers natin.

**[04:07:29.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-003396)  
Anyway, sige.

**[04:07:31.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-003397)

**[04:07:41.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-003398)  
Okay.

**[04:07:43.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-003400)

**[04:08:01.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-003401)

**[04:09:28.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003414)

**[04:09:31.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003415)

ito ang tanong.

**[04:13:28.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003466)

**[04:13:31.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003467)

**[04:13:32.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003468)

**[04:13:49.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003471)

May tatlo pong pinanggagalingan ng problema, Mr. President, at yung tatlong din iyon, posibleng panggagalingan ng solusyon.

**[05:32:57.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004647)  
Una pong source ng deception dito sa pag-issue at paggamit ng persons with disability ID ay sa mga doktor.

**[05:33:09.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004648)  
Na maaaring may doktor na kilala, yung magpapanggap na person with disability.

**[05:33:15.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004649)  
At sa doktor na iyon, kukuha ng certification na may disability siya kahit wala.

**[05:33:26.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004650)

**[05:33:35.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004651)  
Tinutukoy po kung anong medical specialist.

**[05:33:39.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004652)  
Medical specialists lamang ang pwedeng mag-issue ng certification of disability para sa taong mag-a-apply para sa person with disability ID.

**[05:33:47.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004653)

**[05:33:57.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004654)

**[05:34:07.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004655)  
And pangatlo,

**[05:34:09.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004656)

**[05:34:14.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004657)

**[05:34:56.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004664)

**[05:35:09.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004665)  
Doon sa mga totoong persons with disability.

**[05:35:13.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004666)  
Batay sa live database na ginagamit ng gobyerno para dito sa unified ID system, Mr. President.

Okay.

**[05:35:27.860] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004668)

**[05:35:39.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004670)  
Thank you very much.

**[05:36:09.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004671)  
Yung isang solusyon na sinusulong po ng DSWD, Mr. President,

**[05:36:17.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004672)  
ay yung 5.6 million na inulat ng DOJ na fake pala na persons with disability IDs,

**[05:36:24.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004673)

**[05:36:30.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004674)  
So gumagawa po sila ng technological na paraan na yung mga establishments

**[05:36:39.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004675)  
na hihingian ng persons with disability discount

**[05:36:44.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004676)

**[05:36:49.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004677)  
hindi po sila makaka-access.

**[05:36:51.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004678)  
Dahil lahat ng mga establishmentong iyon ay in-encourage ng department

**[05:36:57.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004679)  
gamitin yung live database para i-validate sa pamamagitan ng QR code lamang

**[05:37:06.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004680)  
makaka-access ang tunay na persons.

**[05:37:09.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004681)  
So ngayon kasi karamihan sa mga fake PWD IDs ay yung nasa plastic laminated lang.

**[05:37:18.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004682)

**[05:37:20.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004683)

**[05:37:27.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004684)

**[05:37:29.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004685)  
That's right, Mr. President.

**[05:37:30.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004686)  
Isa-scan lamang, then yun yung mabavalidate on the live database

**[05:37:35.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004687)  
kung tunay na ID ito ng totoong person with disability.

**[05:37:39.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004688)

**[05:37:41.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004689)

**[05:37:52.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004692)

**[05:37:58.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004693)  
Kaya binibigyan nalang, pinapasa agad.

**[05:38:00.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004694)  
So how do we address these 5.6 million of fake PWD IDs?

**[05:38:06.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004695)

**[05:38:17.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004697)

**[05:38:28.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004698)  
Kasi ang nawawala pala, 88.2 billion pesos every year sa mga unpaid taxes.

**[05:38:35.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004699)

**[05:38:44.780] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004702)

Yes, Mr. President.

**[05:48:37.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004879)  
Nung August 3

**[05:48:39.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004880)  
this year, I filed

**[05:48:41.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004881)  
Senate Bill No. 2371

**[05:48:43.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004882)  
seeking to amend the existence of

**[05:48:45.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004883)  
Juvenile Justice and Welfare Act

**[05:48:47.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004884)  
with the following salient provisions.

**[05:48:50.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004885)  
All bahay pag-asa should be

**[05:48:51.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004886)  
under the direct control and supervision

**[05:48:53.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004887)  
funding and management of the

**[05:48:55.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004888)  
DSWD. Para wala naman

**[05:48:57.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004889)  
LGU-LGU na sinasabi nyo.

**[05:48:59.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004890)  
Anina, established regional

**[05:49:01.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004891)  
rehabilitation center, bahay pag-asa per region.

**[05:49:03.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004892)  
For heinous crime, the children should be

**[05:49:05.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004893)  
committed directly to the regional center.

**[05:49:08.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004894)  
Dapat hiwala yung mga rooms, depende sa

**[05:49:09.600] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004895)  
gravity ng crime.

**[05:49:11.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004896)  
Heinous crimes must be confined separately

**[05:49:13.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004897)  
from petty crimes.

**[05:49:14.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004898)  
Yung sinasabi ko noon pa, na dapat

**[05:49:16.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004899)  
yung mga bata na nag-commit ng heinous crime,

**[05:49:19.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004900)  
iba dapat yung kanilang pasilidad.

**[05:49:21.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004901)  
Hiwala dapat sila doon sa mga bata na

**[05:49:23.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004902)  
petty crimes lang

**[05:49:25.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004903)  
kumit. Kasi pag pinagsama-sama mo sila,

**[05:49:26.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004904)  
baka mahawaan itong mga bata

**[05:49:28.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004905)  
na nag-commit.

**[05:49:29.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004906)  
Yung heinous crime, matuturoan sila ng kalokohan.

**[05:49:32.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004907)  
So, mas maganda kung paghiwala yun

**[05:49:33.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004908)  
yung pasilidad

**[05:49:35.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004909)  
ng heinous crimes versus mga petty crimes

**[05:49:37.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-004910)  
ng mga bata,

**[05:49:39.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-004911)

**[06:26:15.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005814)

**[06:34:30.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005876)

**[06:34:35.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005877)

**[06:34:42.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005878)

**[06:34:48.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005879)  
at sa private sector organizations, Mr. President.

**[06:34:53.100] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005880)

**[06:34:59.020] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005881)

**[06:35:23.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005883)

**[06:35:29.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005884)

**[06:35:33.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005885)

**[06:35:44.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005886)

**[06:35:49.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005887)

Pero ginagawa po namin.

**[06:36:52.640] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005904)  
Kasi nanginiwala kami sa walk the extra mile.

**[06:36:58.600] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005905)

**[06:37:01.940] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005907)  
Dahil napakadami pong nakakulong ngayon.

**[06:37:05.160] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005908)  
Ng matatanda.

**[06:37:07.240] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005909)  
Na wala na pong pupuntahan.

**[06:37:09.180] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005910)  
Wala silang.

**[06:37:10.400] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005911)

**[06:37:12.200] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005912)  
Kasi wala silang pupuntahan.

**[06:37:16.160] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005914)

**[06:37:19.660] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005916)

**[06:37:27.400] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005918)

Pero alam nyo.

**[06:37:32.400] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005921)  
Kung may nakausap siya.

**[06:37:34.180] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005922)

**[06:37:35.700] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005923)

I'm sure po Mr. President.

Para maidagdag.

PDL po.

Sorry.

**[06:38:31.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005943)  
Senior persons deprived of liberty, Mr. President.

**[06:38:38.560] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005945)  
Kunti na lang.

**[06:38:46.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005946)  
Mr. President, nabanggit ko na rin po kanina na yung isa sa mga private sector organizations

**[06:38:55.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005947)

**[06:38:57.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005948)  
Yung senior citizens ay yung Integrated Bar of the Philippines.

**[06:39:01.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005949)  
Dahil sa partnership pong iyon, laan po sila na asistihan yung mga senior persons deprived of liberty sa kanilang mga kaso po, Mr. President?

**[06:39:12.520] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005950)  
May isang kahilingan na lang po ako.

**[06:39:15.800] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005951)  
Pwede po bang magsagawa ang DSWD ng isang proposal ng isang...

**[06:39:24.120] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005952)  
Baka po sakaling isang araw mapondohan natin.

**[06:39:27.400] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005953)

**[06:40:04.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005955)

**[06:40:14.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005956)

**[06:40:23.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005957)

**[06:40:27.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005958)
