**[06:40:35.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005959)  
At ito po yung...

**[06:40:36.120] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005960)  
Ah, okay.

**[06:40:37.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005961)  
Sinabit na po nila P249.5 million para sa phase one construction ng home for the elderly sa Oasis Village, Davao de Oro,

**[06:40:47.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005962)  
na ang lupa po para dito ay dinonate na po sa department ng LGU, Mr. President.

**[06:40:52.920] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005963)  
Opo, pakibigay na lang po sa amin na may brother...

**[06:40:56.280] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005964)  
Magsasabit po sila.

**[06:40:57.400] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005965)  
Mr. President.

**[06:40:59.160] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005966)  
Last na lang po ito. Opo, kasi nakatingin na po sa akin si Sen. Joel.

**[06:41:05.880] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005967)  
Last na po ito.

**[06:41:08.840] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005968)  
Thank you, parang ikaw. Thank you.

**[06:41:10.840] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005969)  
Yung pong mga... Marami po kasing tayong inaalagaan ng mga naging biktima po noong shooting sa Tacloban po,

**[06:41:20.820] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005970)  
dyan po sa... Ngayon po, kabisita namin yung sa South Cotabato.

**[06:41:26.280] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005971)  
Ah...

**[06:41:27.020] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005972)  
May mga tanong lang po ako na ilang beses po ba yung support sa kanila?

**[06:41:31.580] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005973)  
Isang beses lang po ba yun? Dalawang beses lang?

**[06:41:33.880] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005974)  
Ano?

**[06:41:49.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005975)  
Tuloy-tuloy po yung case management, Mr. President.

**[06:41:54.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005976)  
Ah...

**[06:41:55.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005977)  
Halimbawa, kamakailan lang...

**[06:41:57.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005978)  
Dumalaw si Secretary sa South Cotabato.

**[06:42:01.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005979)  
At doon, of course, yung psychosocial therapy or intervention ay tuloy-tuloy.

**[06:42:09.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005980)  
Nagkaroon ng financial assistance.

**[06:42:12.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005981)  
Pero kung anumang mga forms ng assistance over the years,

**[06:42:15.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005982)  
yan ay sa pamamagitan po ng case management ng assigned social worker.

**[06:42:21.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005983)  
At pwede rin pong i-submit ng department sa good gentleman.

**[06:42:25.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005984)  
Yung mga assistance provided.

**[06:42:27.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005985)  
As of October 2026,

**[06:42:29.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005986)  
dun po sa mga victim-survivors ng nihilistic violent extremism.

**[06:42:33.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005987)  
May breakdown po ng financial assistance, psychological first aid,

**[06:42:38.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005988)  
provision of meals, at saka care for the carers din po

**[06:42:42.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005989)  
sa mga violent incidents sa Tacloban, sa Zamboanga, at saka sa South Cotabato.

**[06:42:50.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005990)  
Pero ang total po ng mga kliyente na na-asistehan na po ng departments,

**[06:42:56.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005991)  
sa apat na klaseng services na ito, sa tatlong areas na ito,

**[06:43:01.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005992)  
ay umaabot na po sila ng 2,587 clients at the cost of 1,057,000 pesos, Mr. President.

**[06:43:12.360] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005993)  
Apo.

**[06:43:13.580] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005994)  
Total, nabanggit niyo po yung psychosocial. Napaka-importante po noon.

**[06:43:19.980] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005995)

**[06:43:24.900] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005996)

**[06:43:27.440] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005997)  
Dahil kayo po ang inaasahan talaga.

**[06:43:29.780] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005998)  
At kami pong lahat ay naniniwala na napakalaki po ng natutulong ng DSWD sa ating mga kababayan.

**[06:43:36.620] Senator Robinhood C. Padilla** (asr-005999)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[06:43:38.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006000)  
Sukaran. Maraming salamat din po, Mr. President.

**[06:43:40.980] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006001)  
Maraming salamat, Senator Robin.

**[06:43:42.820] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006002)  
At nagpapasalamat po ako sa inyo sa inyong pagmamahal at concern sa ating mga senior citizens,

**[06:43:51.760] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006003)  
lalo na yung mga na-abandonadong senior citizens.

In fact,

**[06:43:56.860] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006005)  
sa Valenzuela, meron ho kaming government-run and finance home for the aged.

**[06:44:03.280] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006006)

**[06:44:17.100] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006009)

**[06:44:22.280] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006010)  
Kaya,

**[06:44:22.840] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006011)

**[06:44:26.860] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006012)  
buong bansa meron eh.

**[06:44:28.380] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006013)  
In fact, nag-file po ako ng batas po dito na palawigin, papadamihin yung ganyang mga home for the aged

**[06:44:34.920] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006014)  
sa lahat po ng parte ng ating bansa.

**[06:44:38.080] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-006015)  
So, tama ho kayo at maraming salamat po sa inyong pagmamahal po sa kanila.

**[07:12:27.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006498)

**[07:12:32.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006499)

**[07:12:34.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006500)

**[07:12:57.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006507)

**[07:12:59.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006508)

**[07:13:00.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006509)  
for 2027,

**[07:13:02.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006510)

**[07:13:08.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006511)

**[07:13:10.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006512)

**[07:13:15.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006513)  
in new appropriations

**[07:13:17.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006514)  
and 668.4 million pesos

**[07:13:20.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006515)

**[07:13:22.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006516)  
The new appropriations are distributed

**[07:13:25.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006517)

**[07:13:26.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006518)  
25.85 billion for the Office of the Secretary,

**[07:13:30.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006519)  
22.60 billion for the Technical Education and Skills Development Authority,

**[07:13:37.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006520)  
2.69 billion for the Professional Regulation Commission,

**[07:13:41.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006521)  
1.93 billion for the National Labor Relations Commission,

**[07:13:46.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006522)  
369.2 million for the National Wages and Productivity Commission,

**[07:13:52.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006523)  
332.5 million for the National Conciliation and Mediation Board,

**[07:13:58.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006524)  
and 78.8 million for the Institute for Labor Studies.

**[07:14:03.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006525)  
Mr. President, distinguished colleagues,

**[07:14:05.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006526)  
the proposal includes a net increase

**[07:14:09.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006527)

to provide free legal assistance to workers

**[07:14:30.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006533)  
20 million, additional 20 million pesos

**[07:14:37.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006535)

**[07:14:53.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006540)  
A net increase of 137.25 million

**[07:14:56.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006541)  
for TESDAS Training for Work Scholarship Program,

**[07:15:08.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006547)  
pesos for free technical

Access to Quality Tertiary

**[07:15:14.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006550)  
Education Act.

**[07:15:16.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006551)

despite our

million pesos po for the hosting

**[07:15:41.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006564)  
Mr. President, in September

**[07:15:43.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006565)  
2026,

**[07:15:45.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006566)  
2.86 million Filipinos

**[07:15:47.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006567)

And employment that remains

Addressing these challenges

Sa budget po na ito, isinusulong

that

we know that the

**[07:17:38.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006622)

**[07:17:40.940] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006623)

Mr. Secretary,

**[07:18:03.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006634)

**[07:18:05.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006635)

**[07:18:10.800] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006636)

**[07:18:14.920] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006638)

**[07:18:18.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006639)

**[07:18:20.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006640)

**[07:18:25.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006642)

**[07:18:27.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-006643)

ay half the daily wage.
